Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the discrete_math structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 251. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 477 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by discrete_math generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 251, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 477. QED.
